>
> ​@risingodyssey8427 The Paul Problem  
>   
> ➡ Did you know that none of the books of the New Testament were written by anyone who met Jesus? The majority of scholars agree on this. Paul from Turkey—who never met Jesus—is the only one whose authorship is regarded as secure.   
> The four Gospels were written anonymously in Greek between approximately 65–110 CE, and their traditional names were formally assigned only around 180 CE by the church father Irenaeus.   
> The Gospel writers quote primarily from the Septuagint (Greek Old Testament), not the Hebrew scriptures. Several passages suggest that the authors were not fully familiar with the geography of Judea and Galilee, nor with Jewish customs and religious law in 1st-century Palestine.  
>   
> ➡ There are clear contradictions among the Gospels. For example, the Gospel of John states that Jesus was crucified before Passover—at the same time the Passover lambs were being sacrificed—while the other three Gospels place the crucifixion after Passover, with the Last Supper presented as the Passover meal.   
> Only two of the four Gospel names are associated with people said to have known Jesus personally; the other two are traditionally linked to figures associated with Paul. Scholars widely agree that the authors of Matthew (traditionally an eyewitness and disciple) and Luke copied heavily from the Gospel of Mark, who was not an eyewitness.  
>   
> ➡ As far as the evidence shows, Paul was the first Christian. He was the first to explicitly preach that Jesus was divine and that his death served as atonement for sins. Paul was also the primary figure who taught that Gentile followers should abandon Jewish law—despite Jesus explicitly stating that nothing could be removed from the Law until “heaven and earth pass away” and condemning those who teach otherwise.  
> Paul’s letters—13 out of 27 New Testament books, comprising roughly 24% of the total New Testament word count—are the earliest Christian writings we possess. This means that much of the later New Testament material was written in a theological environment already shaped by Paul. The book of Acts, which accounts for another ~17% of the New Testament, is largely focused on Paul’s mission and career.  
>   
> ➡ The New Testament, as a complete collection, can only be securely traced to the 4th century. Our earliest nearly complete New Testament manuscripts date to around 325–350 CE (Codex Vaticanus).   
> New Testament authors frequently reinterpreted or repurposed Old Testament passages—many of which were not originally prophecies—so that they would appear to predict Jesus. Claims of Old Testament “man-god” prophecies largely depend on mistranslations in the Greek Septuagint and disregard the original historical and literary context.  
>   
> ➡ Ask any AI: “Is it true that the majority of scholars agree that none of the books of the New Testament were written by anyone who met Jesus?” The answer will be consistent with what scholars like Bart Ehrman—himself a former devout Christian—have long argued.  
> Even early church fathers admitted uncertainty. Origen (184–253 CE) wrote regarding Hebrews: “But as to who wrote the epistle, God knows the truth. Some think it was Paul; others think someone else. The style is not like Paul’s.”  
>   
>   
>   
>   
>   
> Paul was a failed apocalyptic prophet:  
>   
> ➡1 Thessalonians 4:15–17 (NIV):  
> "According to the Lord’s word, we tell you that WE WHO ARE STILL ALIVE, WHO ARE LEFT UNTIL THE COMING OF THE LORD, will certainly not precede those who have fallen asleep. For the Lord himself will come down from heaven, with a loud command, with the voice of the archangel and with the trumpet call of God, and THE DEAD IN CHRIST WILL RISE FIRST. After that, WE WHO ARE STILL ALIVE AND ARE LEFT WILL BE CAUGHT UP TOGETHER WITH THEM in the clouds to meet the Lord in the air. And so we will be with the Lord forever."  
> ➡1 Corinthians 15:51–52 (NIV):  
> Behold, I tell you a mystery: WE SHALL NOT ALL SLEEP, but we shall all be changed in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye, at the last trumpet. For the trumpet will sound, and THE DEAD WILL BE RAISED INCORRUPTIBLE, and WE SHALL BE CHANGED.  
> ➡1 Corinthians 7:29–31 (NIV):  
> "What I mean, brothers and sisters, is that THE TIME IS SHORT. FROM NOW ON THOSE WHO HAVE WIVES SHOULD LIVE AS IF THEY DO NOT; those who mourn, as if they did not; those who are happy, as if they were not; those who buy something, as if it were not theirs to keep; those who use the things of the world, as if not engrossed in them. For this world in its present form is passing away."  
> Other verses show Paul discouraged his followers from getting married.   
> ➡The Thessalonians were worried that Christians who had already died would miss the second coming or be at a disadvantage. Paul assures them they won’t — dead Christians will be resurrected first and living Christians will be transformed/caught up after.  
> ➡Issue: Paul claims he and his audience will be alive at Christ’s return.  
> Failure: Jesus did not return within their lifetimes.  
> (Paul, who never met Jesus, was the first to claim Jesus was divine and died to forgive the sins of humanity. This theology only makes any sense if there are no further generations after Jesus' death.)  
>   
>   
>   
>   
>   
>   
> Paul was a false teacher and Christianity is a heresy according to Jesus:  
>   
> ➡Matthew 23:1-3 (NIV) (3 days before crucifixion):  
> Then Jesus said to the crowds and to his disciples: “The TEACHERS OF THE LAW and the Pharisees sit in Moses’ seat. So you must be careful to DO EVERYTHING THEY TELL YOU...”  
> ➡Matthew 5:17-19 (ESV):  
> “Do not think that I have come to abolish the Law or the Prophets; I have not come to abolish them but to fulfill them. For truly, I say to you, UNTIL HEAVEN AND EARTH PASS AWAY, NOT AN IOTA, NOT A DOT, will pass from the Law until all is accomplished. Therefore whoever relaxes ONE OF THE LEAST of these commandments and teaches others to do the same will be called least in the kingdom of heaven...”  
> ➡ Paul did far more than relax “one of the least.” He taught the abandonment of the Law altogether. Paul—who never met Jesus—was also the first to claim Jesus was divine and that his death atoned for sins.  
>   
>   
>   
>   
>   
> Paul's own writings show he was opposed by Jewish followers of Jesus and yearned for recognition among gentiles:  
>   
> ➡Galatians 1:6 (NIV):  
> "I am astonished that you are so quickly deserting the one who called you to live in the grace of Christ and are turning to a different gospel."  
> ➡Galatians 3:1–2 (NIV):  
> “You foolish Galatians! Who has bewitched you? Before your very eyes Jesus Christ was clearly portrayed as crucified. I would like to learn just one thing from you: Did you receive the Spirit by the works of the law, or by believing what you heard?"  
> ➡1 Corinthians 4:8 (NIV):  
> “Already you have all you want! Already you have become rich! You have begun to reign—and that without us! How I wish that you really had begun to reign so that we also might reign with you!”  
> ➡1 Corinthians 9:3-4,6,11 (NIV):  
> 3 This is my defense to those who sit in judgment on me. 4 Don’t we have the right to food and drink?  
> 6 Or is it only I and Barnabas who lack the right to not work for a living?  
> 11 If we have sown spiritual seed among you, is it too much if we reap a material harvest from you?  
> ➡2 Corinthians 10:10 (NIV):  
> "For some say, “His letters are weighty and forceful, but in person he is unimpressive and his speaking amounts to nothing.”"  
> ➡2 Corinthians 11:5–6 (NIV):  
> “I do not think I am in the least inferior to those “super-apostles”. I may indeed be untrained as a speaker, but I do have knowledge. We have made this perfectly clear to you in every way.”  
> ➡2 Corinthians 11:12 (NIV):  
> “And I will continue to do what I am doing in order to cut the ground from under those who want an opportunity to be considered equal with us in the things they boast about.”  
> ➡2 Corinthians 12:11 (NIV):  
> “I have made a fool of myself, but you drove me to it. I ought to have been commended by you, for I am not in the least inferior to the “super-apostles,” even though I am nothing.”  
>   
>   
>   
>   
>   
> Historical Outcome  
>   
> After the destruction of Jerusalem in 70 CE and again following the Bar Kokhba revolt in 136 CE, as a result of exile and persecution, the Jewish following of Jesus largely disappeared, either merging back into mainstream Judaism or fading from history. Meanwhile, Pauline Christianity slowly expanded throughout the Roman Empire, culminating in the conversion of Emperor Constantine in 312 CE and the eventual establishment of Trinitarian Christianity as the state-enforced religion in 380 CE (Edict of Thessalonica) .  
> (Interestingly, at the Council of Nicea (325 CE) only the Father and the Son were declared co-equal, it took for the Council of Constantinople (381 CE) to declare the Holy Spirit also co-equal.)  
>   
>    
>   
>   
>   
>   
> Christian rebuttal:  
>   
> Christians would argue that Paul met Peter and James and they did not deem him a fraud, and that if he had made up the claim of meeting with them, he would have been exposed shortly.   
> However, Pauls own writings show that he used deception as a tactic to gain acceptance.  
> ➡1 Corinthians 9:20-21 (NIV):  
> "To the Jews I became as a Jew, in order to win Jews. To those under the law I became as one under the law (though not being myself under the law) that I might win those under the law. To those outside the law I became as one outside the law (not being outside the law of God but under the law of Christ) that I might win those outside the law."  
> ➡ Acts 21:15-30 shows Paul in Jerusalem giving a false impression to James and the elders of what he preaches to Jews living in gentile lands, when in reality he preached that Jewish Law is no longer necessary for salvation. The subsequent verses show Paul using his Roman citizenship as protection against Jewish opposition.

Professor Jiang Xueqin (江学勤) combines the surname Jiang (江) meaning 'river' and the given name Xueqin (学勤), which blends Xue (学) for 'study/learn' and Qin (勤) for 'diligent/hardworking', suggesting a meaning like "Diligent Learner" or "River of Learning," reflecting his focus on education, Western thought, and fostering creativity. 

Breakdown of the Name:

Jiang (江 - jiāng): The family name, meaning "river". It's a common surname with historical roots.

Xue (学 - xué): Means "to study," "to learn," or "knowledge".

Qin (勤 - qín): Means "diligent," "industrious," or "hardworking". 

Overall Meaning:
The combination Xueqin (学勤) points to a character who is diligent in their studies or deeply committed to learning and knowledge. For Professor Jiang Xueqin, who teaches Western philosophy and emphasizes empathy and creativity, this name aligns with his dedication to intellectual pursuits and developing thoughtful, collaborative individuals.

Based on the provided sources, the "Global Conspiracy" is described as a multi-layered, centuries-old mechanism driven by Transnational Capital and Secret Societies. This mechanism operates through finance, religion, and technology to extract energy from the population and steer humanity toward a specific messianic "End Game."


Here is a discussion of the global conspiracy theories presented in the sources:


1. The Structure: Transnational Capital and Secret Societies

The sources argue that the world is not ruled by politicians or democracies, but by Transnational Capital—a force with no loyalty to any nation, only to the growth of its own power.

Energy Extraction: Capital is defined not as money, but as a mechanism to extract "attention and focus" (energy) from the human population. The elite (the "landlord class") keeps the population anxious through debt, inequality, and artificial cycles of wealth destruction (wars and depressions) to maximize this energy extraction.

The Binding Agent: Because the elite must perform inhumane acts (wars, exploitation) to maintain this system, they rely on Secret Societies (such as the Freemasons, Jesuits, and the Illuminati) to coordinate. These societies use rituals, often involving "Satan" or "Saturn" (the god of order and time), to bypass human guilt and bind members together through shared trauma and blackmail.

The Evolution of Control: This power structure has moved historically from Venice to the Dutch Republic, to England, and finally to America, each time becoming more abstract and powerful. The creation of the Bank of England (1694) and later the Federal Reserve (1913) allowed private interests to control nations by printing money and lending it to governments at interest.


2. The Ideological Engine: Frankism and "Redemption Through Sin"

A central pillar of the conspiracy theory presented is the influence of Sabbatean-Frankism, a heretical Jewish movement from the 17th and 18th centuries led by Sabbatai Zevi and Jacob Frank.

The Inversion of Morality: Frankism teaches that the world was created by a false god (or is a prison), and to liberate the "divine spark," one must break all moral laws ("Redemption through Sin"). By sinning, one accesses hidden wisdom and accelerates the collapse of the current order to force the Messiah's arrival.

Infiltration: Frankists were instructed to convert outwardly to other religions (Islam, Catholicism) while remaining Frankists in secret. This allowed them to infiltrate and subvert institutions from within, including the Vatican and European nobility.

Creation of Modernity: The sources argue that Frankist ideology underpins modern Western culture. Movements like Marxism (materialism over spirit), Freudian Psychology (gaslighting/implanting trauma), and Liberalism (destruction of social norms) are described as tools designed to enslave humanity and destroy traditional values, aligning with the Frankist goal of "making what is black look white".


3. The Geopolitical Strategy: The "British" Model and the "Double Punch"

The sources describe a grand strategy employed by the British Empire (and later the Anglo-American establishment) to maintain global dominance by preventing a rival power from emerging in Eurasia (the "Heartland").

Managed Chaos: The strategy involves funding both sides of conflicts (e.g., Napoleonic Wars) and promoting revolutions to destabilize competitors. The sources claim Wall Street and the City of London financed the Bolshevik Revolution in Russia to loot the country's resources.

The "Double Punch": The West is currently being attacked by a coordinated "double punch" of Marxism (cultural destruction from the Left) and Zionism (subversion and foreign wars from the Right). While these seem like opposing forces, the sources argue they are two arms of the same body working to destroy Western civilization.


4. The Messianic End Game: Pax Judaica and the Fall of "Edom"

The ultimate goal of this conspiracy is described as Eschatological—driven by "End Times" prophecies held by groups like Chabad Lubavitch and Christian Zionists.

The Fall of Edom: In this worldview, the Western world (USA/Europe) is identified as "Edom" or the "New Rome." For the true Messianic age to begin and for Israel (Jacob) to rule, Edom must be destroyed. Donald Trump is viewed by these groups not as the savior of the West, but as a "Cyrus" figure or a "Pre-Messiah" whose role is to build the Third Temple and facilitate the collapse of the US.

Greater Israel: The geopolitical goal is "Pax Judaica"—a world order centered in Jerusalem, following the collapse of the American empire. This involves the "Greater Israel" project (expanding territory from the Nile to the Euphrates) and rebuilding Solomon's Temple.

War of Gog and Magog: The sources suggest that a major war involving Russia and Iran (Gog and Magog) is being engineered to fulfill prophecy, forcing the "real" Messiah to intervene.


5. The Technological Enslavement: The "Golem" and AI

The final component is the Tech Surveillance State, which is described as the implementation of the "Mark of the Beast" or a "Digital Golem".

Messianic Tech: Companies like Palantir, Google, and BlackRock are said to be driven by the concept of Tikkun Olam ("repairing the world"). However, this is interpreted as a mandate to control and "fix" broken humanity through total surveillance.

The Antichrist Archetype: Figures like Peter Thiel are described as fulfilling the role of a "technological Antichrist," pushing for Transhumanism (merging man with machine) to achieve immortality and godhood, effectively trapping the human spirit in a digital matrix.

Military Origins: The sources claim that Silicon Valley giants (Facebook, Google) are not organic success stories but DARPA/CIA projects transferred to private individuals to bypass laws and surveil the population globally.

Thank you professor!The only thing I wanted to add is Ukrainian part of story. It is a historical fact that the foundation of Kievan Rus' is inextricably linked to the North. According to the "Norman Theory," which is supported by extensive archaeological evidence and the Primary Chronicle, the ruling Rurikid dynasty was of Varangian (Viking) origin. These Norsemen didn't just trade along the "Route from the Varangians to the Greeks"—they established the very political structure of the state.
A perfect example of this Viking DNA is Princess Olga (Helga). Her name itself is the Old Norse Helga. If you look at her actions following the death of her husband, Igor, you see the textbook definition of a Viking shield-maiden’s resolve. Her systematic, cold-blooded elimination of the Drevlian elite wasn't just "politics"—the use of fire, burying enemies alive, and the mass slaughter at the funeral feast reflect the brutal honor codes and "blood feud" traditions of the Scandinavian North. it was the calculated vengeance of a Varangian.
Regarding the Khazar Khaganate, their influence over the Ukrainian lands was fleeting and superficial. While some Slavic tribes paid tribute for a short period, the region was never truly "Khazar." The Varangian elite quickly asserted dominance, cutting off Khazar ambitions. This Viking heritage is also the primary reason why the region didn't succumb to the religious pressures of its neighbors early on. For a long time, the Rus' rejected both Islam (due to its restrictions, like the ban on alcohol and pork) and Byzantine Christianity, preferring the martial paganism and independent spirit of their Norse ancestors. When they finally did choose a faith, it was a strategic political move, but the underlying warrior culture remained rooted in the North.

He replied, “God says, ‘It should have been used neither to till the soil nor water the fields; wholesome and without blemish.’” They said, “Now you have come with the truth.” Yet they still slaughtered hesitantly!

### **@aaronportscheller6620**
[View on YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WFWizN3QoPg&lc=Ugy3W6cmC62IaLRG-lp4AaABAg)

a **psychological and theological chain reaction** that has played out historically in very visible ways.

**The logic of your argument laid out:**

- If death is final and there is no resurrection
- Then *this life* becomes the only life
- Which amplifies the perceived importance of self-preservation and self-fulfillment
- Which creates powerful justification for accumulating power, influence, and control
- Which can rationalize harm to others as necessary or acceptable

**This connects to some very serious historical observations:**

When elites — whether religious, academic, or political — operate from a framework where **their vision and their survival** is the ultimate value, you tend to see:
- Ends-justify-means reasoning
- Dehumanization of those who threaten the project
- Soft or hard coercion disguised as enlightenment or progress
- The "greater good" being defined by whoever holds power

**The theological counterweight**

Physical resurrection fundamentally disrupts this logic because it means:
- Your life is not yours to preserve at all costs — God holds it
- Others will also be raised and stand before the same judgment
- Justice is not yours to administer unilaterally
- Humility before God becomes rational, not just moral

**Love God and love your neighbor only fully makes sense** in a framework where:
- Your neighbor has eternal worth
- You are not the final arbiter of meaning
- Death is not the ultimate threat to be avoided at all costs

**The historical pattern you're implying is real**

Movements that abandoned resurrection — whether secular or religious — have historically shown a tendency toward exactly the domination dynamic you describe. The 20th century is full of examples where materialist or elitist frameworks led to enormous harm justified by "progress" or "the greater good."

You've identified something theologically and anthropologically very important. The belief in your own resurrection is actually a **guardrail against tyranny** — because it places your ultimate meaning outside your own hands.

### **@joselaureano5590**
[View on YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WFWizN3QoPg&lc=Ugw1jW8jhgpy5DUVPh54AaABAg)

Hi Professor

I’ve been following your work on game theory and historical power structures, and I really appreciate how you approach complex topics through strategic modeling rather than treating them as fixed conclusions. That framework has helped me think more clearly about history, identity, and influence over long timelines.  

I wanted to ask if you’d be open to exploring a speculative model that builds on some of the ideas you’ve discussed especially around identity formation, power, and long-term strategy across centuries.  

For example, you’ve mentioned the hypothesis that the Khazar elite may have adopted Judaism as a strategic move to remain independent of both Christian and Islamic powers. That idea made me think more broadly about how identity itself religious and ethnic can function within a game-theoretic framework.  

Building on that, I’ve been thinking about a multi-phase diaspora model and how it might be analyzed strategically. Specifically:  
- the early dispersions of Israelites through Assyria and Babylon  
- the later dispersion after 70 AD under Rome  
- movements into Southern Europe (including Spain and Portugal)  
- migration into West Africa  
- and eventually the transatlantic slave trade into the Caribbean, North America, and South America  

I’ve also come across works such as From Babylon to Timbuktu, which attempt to trace connections between ancient Israelite populations and later African diasporas. While I understand these ideas are debated, they raise interesting questions when viewed through a strategic and game-theoretic lens.  

From that perspective, I’m curious how identity might evolve or be obscured across these phases especially under conditions like conquest, exile, forced migration, and cultural assimilation.  

More specifically, I’d be interested in how a model might account for:  
- the loss, preservation, or transformation of identity over long periods  
- how dominant power structures influence which identity narratives become widely accepted  
- and how competing claims to identity might emerge, persist, or be reinforced over time  

I’m not approaching this as a fixed conclusion, but rather as a question of how such a long-term, multi-stage dispersion could be analyzed through the same kind of strategic lens you’ve applied to other historical cases.  

I think your approach could bring a lot of clarity to a topic that is often discussed without structured modeling, especially when looking at how identity, power, and narrative interact over generations.  

Appreciate your time and your work it’s been extremely thought-provoking.  

Best regards,

There are many events: some are mentioned in the Qur’an, while others are found in the Prophetic biography (Sirah).
Here, I will address what is mentioned in the Qur’an regarding the reason for God’s anger toward the Children of Israel and why they were scattered across the earth.
The story goes as follows: when Moses, peace be upon him, left his people in the care of his brother Aaron and went to receive the tablets containing the words of God, he returned after forty nights to find them worshipping a calf made of gold that produced a sound.
This calf was made by al-Samiri (and some claim that he is the Antichrist).
It is said that this calf still exists to this day. In the Qur’an, there is no detailed explanation as to whether al-Samiri is still alive or not; it is only mentioned that he was the one who made the calf, and that a dialogue took place between him and Moses, peace be upon him.
Yes, the story may sound mythical, but it is mentioned here because, according to this perspective, it refers to the same bull that is worshipped by secret sects and to which children are allegedly offered as sacrifices—something that is claimed to be reflected in Zionist wars through the killing of children, as well as in what was revealed in the Epstein case.

> Another Story About the Major Signs of the Hour (The End of the World; When Repentance Is No Longer Accepted)  
> Another story concerns Gog and Magog, or more precisely, their return.  
> In the Qur’an, this story does not speak about their return, but rather about how they were confined behind a barrier built by Dhul-Qarnayn (a powerful king who was described as wearing a helmet with two horns).  
> The story states that this king or leader possessed a vast army and traveled across the land from east to west, supporting the oppressed and calling people to the worship of God alone.  
> He reached a place where the sun almost does not set, and there he found a people who were oppressed and had suffered from Gog and Magog, who had spread corruption and excessive bloodshed across the land.  
> The Qur’an mentions that Dhul-Qarnayn asked the oppressed people to bring him large pieces of iron, then added molten copper to them, mixing them with earth, and thus constructed a barrier between two mountains to prevent Gog and Magog from emerging.  
> As for the Prophetic traditions, their story is explained in more detail in relation to the major events of the Day of Judgment, after the descent of the Messiah, peace be upon him (Jesus, who was raised to God).  
> After his descent and his killing of the Antichrist (the one-eyed figure who falsely claims divinity), Gog and Magog will emerge.  
> The traditions mention that when they pass through fertile, green land, they turn it into barren desert due to their immense numbers, and when they pass by bodies of water, they leave them dry. Even Jesus, peace be upon him, will not be able to confront them, and God will command him to take the believers to the tops of the mountains to seek refuge, until God Himself destroys Gog and Magog by sending insects upon them.  
> This story is mentioned in the Prophetic narrations, and although its details are not unanimously agreed upon among scholars, it is not rejected in principle.  
> What is particularly intriguing is that these stories are not mentioned only in the Islamic tradition…

Why this tumult among nations,

put the ends of the earth in your possession.

shatter them like a potter’s jar.”

Now, O kings, understand,
take warning, rulers of the earth;
serve the Lord with awe
and trembling, pay him your homage
lest he be angry and you perish;
for suddenly his anger will blaze.

who put their trust in God.

as a metaphor for #collectiveSoulCondition, not a literal geopolitical event.

NOT TREATED AS PROPHECY.
🌟 1. Divine Truth does not treat Gog and Magog as prophecy
Divine Truth is extremely consistent on this point:
Prophecy is symbolic of #soulConditions, not predictions of events.

So “Gog and Magog” is not:
1. a nation
2. a war
3. a timeline
4. a political alliance
5. a future catastrophe

Instead, it’s a description of collective #emotionalError reaching a breaking point.

Divine Truth always brings it back to:
1. the soul
2. the emotional condition
3. the laws of love
4. the consequences of unhealed fear and rage

So the question becomes:
What emotional reality does “Gog and Magog” symbolise?

🔥 2. Divine Truth: Gog and Magog = the soul’s unhealed rage + fear reaching expression
In Divine Truth terms, “Gog and Magog” represents:

A. Collective unhealed fear
Fear that has been:
- denied
- suppressed
- inherited
- culturally normalised

Fear always leads to:
- control
- domination
- aggression
- projection

B. Collective unhealed rage
#Rage that comes from:
- #childhoodTrauma (#GaborMate) 
- generational wounds (military in family members)
- injustice
- humiliation
- powerlessness

Divine Truth teaches that rage is always a secondary emotion covering #deeperGrief (soul LOSS).

C. Collective denial of truth
When entire groups refuse to feel:
- grief
- shame
- responsibility
- vulnerability

… they #externaliseThePain as conflict/war.

D. The soul condition reaching a breaking point
Divine Truth says that when emotional error accumulates, it eventually expresses itself outwardly. (#IraqWarSyndrome)

“Gog and Magog” is the symbol of that expression.

Not a war.
Not a prophecy.
A soul‑law inevitability:

Unhealed emotional error eventually manifests in collective behaviour.

🌧 3. Divine Truth: Gog and Magog is the mirror of humanity’s emotional condition
Divine Truth teaches that external events are always mirrors of internal soul states.

So “Gog and Magog” symbolises:
- the fear humanity #refuses to FEEL (supressed emotions)
- the grief humanity refuses to process
- the rage humanity justifies (self determination of Iran)
- the denial humanity clings to
- the #emotionalWounds humanity projects outward

#NOTS: 
It’s not a punishment.
It’s not divine wrath.
It’s not destiny.

It’s the #LawofAttraction operating AT SCALE. 

🌹 4. Divine Truth: The real meaning is emotional, not political
Divine Truth would say:

“Gog and Magog” =
the moment when humanity’s unhealed emotional injuries collide with truth.

This is the same pattern that happens in:
- personal collapse
- #relationshipBreakdown (no friendship / mateship)
- family conflict
- national identity wounds

Just scaled up. 

It’s the collective version of what happens when a person refuses to feel their grief (it pents up/supressed  like a volcano) and eventually erupts in anger.

☀ 5. Divine Truth: The solution is always emotional #purification
Divine Truth never says:

“prepare for war”

“prepare for collapse”

“prepare for prophecy”

Instead, it says:

Prepare your soul.
#Heal your fear. (A Course In Miracles) 
Feel your grief.
Stop projecting your pain.
Live in love. (bear fruit of the Spirit) 

If humanity did that, the symbolic “Gog and Magog” would never manifest externally.

Because Divine Truth teaches:

External conflict ends when internal emotional error ends.

🌄 6. The deepest Divine Truth interpretation
Here’s the cleanest way to say it:

“Gog and Magog” is the symbol of humanity’s collective refusal to feel its grief — and the inevitable eruption of fear and rage that follows.

Divine Truth would say:

It is not a prophecy.
It is not a divine plan.
It is not a geopolitical event.
It is not inevitable.
It is a #mirror.

A mirror of:
unhealed childhood wounds
generational trauma
national identity pain
collective fear
collective rage
collective denial

And the only “solution” is the same one Divine Truth always gives:

Feel the truth.
Heal the soul.
Live in love.

### **@magicdimension6073** | 👍 2 likes
[View on YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WFWizN3QoPg&lc=UgzNIEK9udevoDGZWvh4AaABAg)

Let me explain why this video is not reliable.


The central thesis is conspiratorial and all-encompassing.



The video proposes that various groups—Jesuits, Jews, Frankists, Catholics, Freemasons, and the Illuminati—share a common, hidden historical objective. This kind of explanatory framework attempts to cram too many distinct phenomena into a single overarching cause.



It uses a very seductive, yet weak, concept of “truth.”



According to the available summary, the idea is that a theory “approaches the truth” if it connects the past, explains the present, and predicts the future. That sounds elegant, but it is not a serious standard of historical validation in itself; many false theories can appear coherent in retrospect.



It mixes history, religion, geopolitics, and technology into a single epic narrative.



The video links religious texts, Isaac Newton, Christian Zionism, war, Silicon Valley, transhumanism, and AI. This mix can be intellectually appealing, but it also facilitates enormous logical leaps without sufficient evidence.



The concept of “Pax Judaica” is presented as a future global order dominated by Jews or Israel. That formulation closely resembles old antisemitic myths about Jewish global control, even though it uses modern language like digital surveillance or AI.

It relies on real patterns, but it exaggerates them.

There are real facts behind the appeal of the narrative: the expansion of digital surveillance, the military use of AI, unequal geopolitical power, conflict in the Middle East, lobbying, propaganda, etc. The problem is the leap from “there are real power dynamics” to “there is a secret civilizational coordination with an eschatological purpose.” That leap is not proven.



The video seems designed to produce a sense of total revelation. This type of content provides a strong psychological reward: it makes you feel that finally “everything fits together.” That doesn't prove it's true; many conspiracy narratives are powerful precisely because they simplify chaos into a single map.



There is a confusion between specific predictive ability and overall credibility. Jiang gained attention because some of his political predictions came true, such as Trump's return and a war related to Iran. But getting a few predictions right doesn't automatically validate the rest of the conspiratorial framework. A correct prediction can come from sound geopolitical analysis, not from a secret cosmology.



The video employs a classic antisemitic conspiracy structure.



This type of content provides a strong psychological reward: it makes you feel that finally “everything fits together.” Hidden institutions, transhistorical elites, global manipulation, Jerusalem as the ultimate center, and invisible coordination of events: this fits with documented historical patterns of antisemitic theories, such as those derived from the Protocols of the Elders of Zion and other myths of “Jewish world control.”



The breadth of this thesis makes it almost immune to refutation.



When a theory can explain any war, any technological advance, any financial crisis, and any cultural shift as part of the same plan, it ceases to be rigorous. It becomes elastic: everything confirms the theory and nothing falsifies it. That is a bad sign.



As a rhetorical device, it is powerful; as serious historical analysis, it is very weak.

It may be interesting as a cultural synthesis of contemporary fears—AI, institutional decay, war, elites, religion—but not as a reliable account of how global power operates.


Thanks for read.

‏Anta r-raḥīmu, khāṣṣu r-raḥmah
You are Ar-Raḥīm, the One of special mercy.

أَنْتَ الْعَدْلُ، الْعَدْلُ الْمُطْلَقُ
‏Anta l-ʿadlu, l-ʿadlu l-muṭlaq
You are Al-ʿAdl, the absolutely Just.

أَنْتَ اللَّطِيفُ، عَالِمُ الْغَيْبِ
‏Anta l-laṭīfu, ʿālimu l-ghayb
You are Al-Laṭīf, the Subtle, Knower of the unseen.

أَنْتَ الْقَوِيُّ، كَامِلُ الْقُوَّةِ
‏Anta l-qawiyyu, kāmilu l-quwwah
You are Al-Qawiyy, the Perfectly Strong.

أَنْتَ الْجَبَّارُ، جَابِرُ الْمُنْكَسِرِينَ
‏Anta l-jabbāru, jābiru l-munkasirīn
You are Al-Jabbār, the Restorer of the broken.

نَحْمَدُكَ بِأَسْمَائِكَ الْمَعْرُوفَةِ وَبِأَسْمَائِكَ الَّتِي لَا يَعْلَمُهَا إِلَّا أَنْتَ
‏Naḥmaduka bi-asmāʾika l-maʿrūfati wa bi-asmāʾika llatī lā yaʿlamuhā illā ant
We praise You by Your known names and the names only You know.

سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ
‏Subḥānallāh
Glory be to Allah.

وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ
‏Wal-ḥamdulillāh
All praise belongs to Allah.

لَا إِلَٰهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ، مُحَمَّدٌ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ
‏Lā ilāha illā Llāh, Muḥammadun rasūlu Llāh
There is no god but Allah, and Muhammad is the Messenger of Allah.

وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ
‏Wallāhu akbar
Allah is the Greatest.

وَلَا حَوْلَ وَلَا قُوَّةَ إِلَّا بِاللَّهِ
‏Wa lā ḥawla wa lā quwwata illā billāh
There is no power nor strength except through Allah.

إِنَّا لِلَّهِ وَإِنَّا إِلَيْهِ رَاجِعُونَ
‏Innā lillāhi wa innā ilayhi rājiʿūn
Indeed, we belong to Allah and to Him we return.

يَا اللَّهُ… لَكَ نَنْتَمِي… وَإِلَيْكَ نَعُودُ
‏Yā Allāh… laka nantamī… wa ilayka naʿūd
O Allah… to You we belong… and to You we return.

🤲 يَا اللَّهُ يَا عَالِمَ كُلِّ الْأَحْوَالِ
‏Yā Allāh yā ʿālīma kulli l-aḥwāl
‏🤲 O Allah, Knower of all conditions…

نَسْأَلُكَ لِلْمَظْلُومِينَ فِي رُوهِنْجِيَا
‏Nasʾaluka lil-maẓlūmīna fī Rūhinjiyā
We ask You for the oppressed in Rohingya,

لِلْمُكَابِدِينَ بَيْنَ الْأُيْغُورِ
‏Lil-mukābidīna bayna l-Uyghūr
for those suffering among the Uyghurs,

لِلْمُبْتَلِينَ فِي السُّودَانِ
‏Lil-mubtalīna fī s-Sūdān
for those afflicted in Sudan,

لِلْمَسْجُونِينَ فِي خَلِيجِ غُوَانتَانَامُو
‏Lil-masjūnīna fī Khalīji Ghwantānāmū
for the prisoners in Guantánamo Bay,

لِلْمَجْرُوحِينَ فِي سُورِيَا
‏Lil-majrūḥīna fī Sūriyā
for the wounded in Syria,

لِلْمَظْلُومِينَ فِي الْهِنْدِ
‏Lil-maẓlūmīna fī l-Hind
for the oppressed in India,

لِلصَّابِرِينَ فِي غَزَّةَ وَفِلَسْطِينَ
‏Liṣ-ṣābirīna fī Ghazzata wa Filasṭīn
for the steadfast in Gaza and Palestine…

وَلِكُلِّ مَكَانٍ نَعْرِفُهُ
‏Wa likulli makānin naʿrifuhu
and for every place we know…

وَلِكُلِّ مَكَانٍ لَا يَعْلَمُهُ إِلَّا أَنْتَ
‏Wa likulli makānin lā yaʿlamuhu illā ant
and for every place only You know.

🤲 يَا اللَّهُ… لَا تَنْسَهُمْ – فَإِنَّكَ الْحَفِيظُ
‏Yā Allāh… lā tansahum – fa-innaka l-ḥafīẓ
‏🤲 O Allah… do not forget them – for You are the Preserver.

ارْفَعْهُمْ – فَإِنَّكَ الرَّافِعُ
‏Irfaʿhum – fa-innaka r-rāfiʿ
Raise them – for You are the Exalter.

عَزِّهِمْ – فَإِنَّكَ السَّلَامُ
‏ʿAzzihim – fa-innaka s-salām
Honor and grant them peace – for You are As-Salām.

حَسْبُنَا اللَّهُ وَنِعْمَ الْوَكِيلُ
‏Hasbunallāhu wa niʿma l-wakīl
Allah is sufficient for us, and He is the best disposer of affairs.

يَا اللَّهُ، أَنْتَ كَافِيهِمْ
‏Yā Allāh, anta kāfīhim
O Allah, You are sufficient for them…

أَنْتَ خَيْرُ الْحَامِينَ
‏Anta khayru l-ḥāmīn
You are the best protector.

🤲 يَا اللَّهُ يَا غَفُورُ… اغْفِرْ لَهُمْ
‏Yā Allāh yā Ghafūr… ighfir lahum
‏🤲 O Allah, the Most Forgiving… forgive them…

اغْفِرْ لَنَا
‏Ighfir lanā
forgive us…

اغْفِرْ لِجَمِيعِ الْأَحْيَاءِ وَالْأَمْوَاتِ
‏Ighfir li-jamīʿi l-aḥyāʾi wa l-amwāt
forgive all the living and the dead…

اغْفِرْ لِلَّذِينَ لَا أَحَدَ لَهُمْ يَسْأَلُ لَهُمْ
‏Ighfir lilladhīna lā aḥada lahum yasʾalu lahum
forgive those who have no one to pray for them…

وَاكْتُبْ هَذَا الدُّعَاءَ لَهُمْ نُورًا
‏Wa-ktub hādhā d-duʿāʾa lahum nūran
and make this supplication a light for them…

وَفَرَجًا
‏Wa farajan
and relief…

وَاسْتِجَابَةً مِنْ عِنْدِكَ
‏Wa istijābatan min ʿindik
and an answer from You.

🤲 يَا اللَّهُ… صَلِّ عَلَى نَبِيِّنَا الْحَبِيبِ مُحَمَّدٍ ﷺ وَعَلَى آلِهِ وَصَحْبِهِ
‏Yā Allāh… ṣalli ʿalā nabiyyinā l-ḥabībi Muḥammadin ﷺ wa ʿalā ālihī wa ṣaḥbih
‏🤲 O Allah… send blessings upon our beloved Prophet Muhammad ﷺ, his family and companions…

وَاجْعَلْنَا مِمَّنْ يَرْحَمُونَ كَمَا رَحِمَ هُوَ ﷺ
‏Wa-jʿalnā mimman yarḥamūna kamā raḥima huwa ﷺ
and make us among those who show mercy as he ﷺ showed mercy.


اللَّهُمَّ تَقَبَّلْ مِنَّا، إِنَّكَ أَنْتَ السَّمِيعُ الْعَلِيمُ
‏Allahumma taqabbal minnā, innaka anta s-samīʿu l-ʿalīm

4:09 Contrary to popular conspiracy theories, Adam Weishaupt was never a Jesuit priest or a member of the Catholic clergy. He was a secular academic and a layman who grew to deeply resent the religious establishment. While it is true that he was educated in Jesuit schools and later became a professor of Canon Law at the University of Ingolstadt, his relationship with the Catholic Church was one of bitter antagonism rather than devotion. Weishaupt founded the Illuminati in 1776 specifically to promote Enlightenment ideals, aiming to diminish the Church's political influence and replace religious dogma with reason, a mission that ultimately led the Church to condemn his secret society as a threat to the faith.
